# Tout ce qu'il faut générer, et comment

Un seul fichier pour produire les images du jeu : les règles communes (section 1), puis **chaque image restante avec son
prompt complet** (section 2), famille par famille, écran par écran, et les visuels de boutique (section 3). Généré par
`node tools/generation-complete.cjs` ; le fichier jumeau `docs/generation-complete.csv` donne la même liste à un
générateur automatique (une ligne par image ou par bande d'animation : fichier, taille, cases, prompt).

## 1. Comment générer

1. **Référence de style** : joindre `tools/repaint/style-ref.png` (un étal de marché égyptien peint) comme **première image**
   de chaque génération. Chaque prompt commence par la phrase de style ci-dessous, puis la phrase de fond, puis la
   consigne de cadrage de la famille, puis le sujet.
2. **Phrase de style** (début de chaque prompt) :
   `Hand-painted isometric game asset in exactly the art style of the first reference image (an Egyptian market stall): clean dark-brown ink outlines, flat painted textures with soft shading and subtle gradients, warm earthy palette with a few saturated accents, small lively details, 2:1 isometric projection viewed from the front at 30 degrees of elevation, light coming from the upper left. No text, no letters, no watermark, no ground tile under the subject.`
3. **Fond** : transparent de préférence (phrase ci-dessous) ; sinon magenta #FF00FF uniforme, détouré à l'import.
   `The image must have a fully transparent background (alpha channel): absolutely nothing painted around the subject, no backdrop, no vignette, no glow, no halo, no gradient. Only the subject and its small cast shadow on the right are opaque.`
4. **Guide de cadrage** : les images marquées « guide : … » ont un rendu 3D provisoire qui fixe l'angle, la place dans le
   cadre et l'emprise ; le joindre comme **deuxième image** avec la phrase « Repaint the subject shown in the second
   image… » (elle est incluse dans leur prompt). Les guides se produisent avec `node tools/repaint.cjs --kit=<dossier>`
   (dossiers `guides/` et `prompts/`). Sans guide, respecter la consigne de composition du prompt.
5. **Compositions par type d'image** (incluses dans chaque prompt) :

| Type | Consigne |
|---|---|
| building | Composition: square image. The building stands on a square ground plot seen as a 2:1 diamond, centered horizontally; the diamond is about 51% of the image width, its center is at 73% of the image height and its bottom corner at 86%; the building rises from the plot toward the top and never goes outside the plot footprint. |
| prop | Composition: square image, the prop centered horizontally, its base at 89% of the image height, about 60% of the width. |
| map | Composition: square image, the subject on a small square ground plot seen as a 2:1 diamond centered horizontally, diamond width about 57% of the image, center at 77% of the height. |
| mapunit | Composition: square image, the marching figures centered, feet at about 77% of the image height, overall height about 45% of the image. |
| figure | Composition: square image, a full-body character centered, feet at 90% of the image height, head near 12%, three-quarter view slightly from above, facing the viewer's right. |
| bust | Composition: square image, head and shoulders filling about 80% of the frame, centered, slightly turned to the right. |
| icon | Composition: square image, a single object centered filling about 70% of the frame, three-quarter view from slightly above. |
| ui | Composition: the element fills the whole square image edge to edge, front view, no perspective. |
| stage | Composition: portrait image (2:3), the hero centered, feet at 90% of the height, standing on a round platform, banners and torches behind. |
| header | Composition: wide image, the scene centered, ground at 70% of the height, nothing cut at the edges. |
| scene | Composition: the panorama fills the whole image, isometric view from the front at 30 degrees, sky transparent above the island. |

6. **Tailles de livraison** : building 1024×1024 · plot 1024×1024 · field 1024×1024 · prop 512×768 · map 1024×1024 · figure 1024×1536 · bust 1024×1024 · icon 1024×1024 · stage 1024×1536 · header 1536×1024 · battlefield 1536×1024 · sceneP 1024×1536 · sceneL 1536×1024 · button 1024×1024 · frame 1024×1024 · plate 1024×1024 · puff 1024×1024 · fx 512×512 · texture 1024×1024 · edge 1024×256 · decal 1024×1024 ; les scènes de connexion et les estrades de héros 1024×1536, les bandeaux et vignettes 1536×1024 ; bannière Google Play 1024×500 ; icône de notification 96×96.
7. **Orientation des bâtiments** : l'entrée principale de chaque bâtiment et de chaque chantier est sur sa face avant droite,
   vers le bas à droite de l'image (sud-est, côté porte de la ville), comme dans les guides 3D ; la phrase est incluse dans leurs prompts.
8. **Nommage** : exactement le nom indiqué, extension `.png`, sous-dossiers respectés (`buildings/ROME/MAIN_HALL.png`).
9. **Animations** : **une seule image par animation**, bande horizontale de N cases égales côte à côte (N = nombre de phases),
   même personnage, même taille, même position dans chaque case, seule la pose change ; cases 512×768 (ville) ou 512×512
   (carte, effets) ; nommer la bande du nom de base sans numéro (`props/villager_ROME_a.png` pour `villager_ROME_a_0..5`),
   elle est découpée à l'import. Phrase incluse dans le prompt : « A sprite sheet: N equal frames side by side… ».
10. **Livraison** : dossier ou zip dans `art/incoming/`, par lots ; vérification `node tools/check-incoming.cjs art/incoming`
   (noms, tailles, transparence, cases des bandes) ; import `node tools/repaint.cjs --import=art/incoming` ; aperçu en jeu
   `node tools/preview-art.cjs <CIV> <biome>`. Ce qui manque garde son rendu 3D ou son dessin provisoire.
11. **Ordre conseillé** : ce qui se voit tout le temps (bâtiments courants, champs, enceinte et portes, sols, interface, carte),
    puis la vie de la ville (échafaudages, ouvriers, villageois, flammes, bâtiments vivants), les systèmes récents (camps,
    colons, technologies, chantiers), les fiches (unités, héros), le reste (paliers 1 et 3, ambiance, météo, douves).

## 2. Ce qu'il faut générer

### Inscription et connexion

#### Scènes de connexion (portrait, paysage) — 2 images

Où : écran de connexion. Priorité ★★★.

Début du prompt, commun à la famille :

```
Hand-painted isometric game asset in exactly the art style of the first reference image (an Egyptian market stall): clean dark-brown ink outlines, flat painted textures with soft shading and subtle gradients, warm earthy palette with a few saturated accents, small lively details, 2:1 isometric projection viewed from the front at 30 degrees of elevation, light coming from the upper left. No text, no letters, no watermark, no ground tile under the subject. The image must have a fully transparent background (alpha channel): absolutely nothing painted around the subject, no backdrop, no vignette, no glow, no halo, no gradient. Only the subject and its small cast shadow on the right are opaque.
Repaint the subject shown in the second image (a plain 3D render used as a layout guide). Keep exactly the same camera angle, the same position in the frame, the same footprint, proportions and overall silhouette as the second image. Do not add anything outside its silhouette except small props at its base and a soft cast shadow to the right.
Composition: the panorama fills the whole image, isometric view from the front at 30 degrees, sky transparent above the island.
```

| Fichier | Taille | Guide | Sujet (fin du prompt, après « Subject: ») |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scenes/auth-landscape.png` | 1536×1024 | rendu 3D `scenes/auth-landscape.png` | a lush floating island diorama seen in isometric view, wide format, with fifteen buildings of six ancient civilisations on grass plots along dirt roads, trees and rocks around the edge, a brown earth cliff under the island |
| `scenes/auth-portrait.png` | 1024×1536 | rendu 3D `scenes/auth-portrait.png` | a lush floating island diorama seen in isometric view, with fifteen buildings of six ancient civilisations (Roman, Gallic, Greek, Egyptian, Hunnic, Sultanate) on grass plots along dirt roads, trees and rocks around the edge, a brown earth cliff under the island |

Début du prompt, commun à la famille :

```
Hand-painted isometric game asset in exactly the art style of the first reference image (an Egyptian market stall): clean dark-brown ink outlines, flat painted textures with soft shading and subtle gradients, warm earthy palette with a few saturated accents, small lively details, 2:1 isometric projection viewed from the front at 30 degrees of elevation, light coming from the upper left. No text, no letters, no watermark, no ground tile under the subject. The image must have a fully transparent background (alpha channel): absolutely nothing painted around the subject, no backdrop, no vignette, no glow, no halo, no gradient. Only the subject and its small cast shadow on the right are opaque.
```
```
Repaint the subject shown in the second image (a plain 3D render used as a layout guide). Keep exactly the same camera angle and the same position in the frame; the building is a smaller, humbler early version, so it may be simpler and lower than the guide, but it must stay centered on the same spot and fit inside the same footprint. Do not add anything outside the footprint except small props at its base and a soft cast shadow to the right.
```
```
Composition: square image. The building stands on a square ground plot seen as a 2:1 diamond, centered horizontally; the diamond is about 51% of the image width, its center is at 73% of the image height and its bottom corner at 86%; the building rises from the plot toward the top and never goes outside the plot footprint.
```

#### Chantiers : chaque bâtiment en construction par civilisation — 168 images

Où : bâtiment en première construction. Priorité ★★☆.

Début du prompt, commun à la famille :

```
Hand-painted isometric game asset in exactly the art style of the first reference image (an Egyptian market stall): clean dark-brown ink outlines, flat painted textures with soft shading and subtle gradients, warm earthy palette with a few saturated accents, small lively details, 2:1 isometric projection viewed from the front at 30 degrees of elevation, light coming from the upper left. No text, no letters, no watermark, no ground tile under the subject. The image must have a fully transparent background (alpha channel): absolutely nothing painted around the subject, no backdrop, no vignette, no glow, no halo, no gradient. Only the subject and its small cast shadow on the right are opaque.
Repaint the subject shown in the second image (a plain 3D render used as a layout guide). Keep exactly the same camera angle, the same position in the frame, the same footprint, proportions and overall silhouette as the second image. Do not add anything outside its silhouette except small props at its base and a soft cast shadow to the right.
Composition: square image. The building stands on a square ground plot seen as a 2:1 diamond, centered horizontally; the diamond is about 51% of the image width, its center is at 73% of the image height and its bottom corner at 86%; the building rises from the plot toward the top and never goes outside the plot footprint.
```

Où : autour de la ville (palier du Bâtiment principal). Priorité ★★★.

Début du prompt, commun à la famille :

```
Hand-painted isometric game asset in exactly the art style of the first reference image (an Egyptian market stall): clean dark-brown ink outlines, flat painted textures with soft shading and subtle gradients, warm earthy palette with a few saturated accents, small lively details, 2:1 isometric projection viewed from the front at 30 degrees of elevation, light coming from the upper left. No text, no letters, no watermark, no ground tile under the subject. The image must have a fully transparent background (alpha channel): absolutely nothing painted around the subject, no backdrop, no vignette, no glow, no halo, no gradient. Only the subject and its small cast shadow on the right are opaque.
Repaint the subject shown in the second image (a plain 3D render used as a layout guide). Keep exactly the same camera angle, the same position in the frame, the same footprint, proportions and overall silhouette as the second image. Do not add anything outside its silhouette except small props at its base and a soft cast shadow to the right.
Composition: square image. The building stands on a square ground plot seen as a 2:1 diamond, centered horizontally; the diamond is about 51% of the image width, its center is at 73% of the image height and its bottom corner at 86%; the building rises from the plot toward the top and never goes outside the plot footprint.
```

```
Hand-painted isometric game asset in exactly the art style of the first reference image (an Egyptian market stall): clean dark-brown ink outlines, flat painted textures with soft shading and subtle gradients, warm earthy palette with a few saturated accents, small lively details, 2:1 isometric projection viewed from the front at 30 degrees of elevation, light coming from the upper left. No text, no letters, no watermark, no ground tile under the subject. The image must have a fully transparent background (alpha channel): absolutely nothing painted around the subject, no backdrop, no vignette, no glow, no halo, no gradient. Only the subject and its small cast shadow on the right are opaque.
Composition: square image. The building stands on a square ground plot seen as a 2:1 diamond, centered horizontally; the diamond is about 51% of the image width, its center is at 73% of the image height and its bottom corner at 86%; the building rises from the plot toward the top and never goes outside the plot footprint.
```

#### Fiches d'unité : une figurine par unité (60) — 60 images

Où : fiche d'unité, carte de recrutement. Priorité ★★☆.

Début du prompt, commun à la famille :

```
Hand-painted isometric game asset in exactly the art style of the first reference image (an Egyptian market stall): clean dark-brown ink outlines, flat painted textures with soft shading and subtle gradients, warm earthy palette with a few saturated accents, small lively details, 2:1 isometric projection viewed from the front at 30 degrees of elevation, light coming from the upper left. No text, no letters, no watermark, no ground tile under the subject. The image must have a fully transparent background (alpha channel): absolutely nothing painted around the subject, no backdrop, no vignette, no glow, no halo, no gradient. Only the subject and its small cast shadow on the right are opaque.
Composition: square image, a full-body character centered, feet at 90% of the image height, head near 12%, three-quarter view slightly from above, facing the viewer's right.
```

#### Fiches d'unité : un jeton par unité (60) — 60 images

Où : listes, rapports, composition d'armée. Priorité ★★☆.

Début du prompt, commun à la famille :

```
Hand-painted isometric game asset in exactly the art style of the first reference image (an Egyptian market stall): clean dark-brown ink outlines, flat painted textures with soft shading and subtle gradients, warm earthy palette with a few saturated accents, small lively details, 2:1 isometric projection viewed from the front at 30 degrees of elevation, light coming from the upper left. No text, no letters, no watermark, no ground tile under the subject. The image must have a fully transparent background (alpha channel): absolutely nothing painted around the subject, no backdrop, no vignette, no glow, no halo, no gradient. Only the subject and its small cast shadow on the right are opaque.
Composition: square image, head and shoulders filling about 80% of the frame, centered, slightly turned to the right.
```

#### Héros sur l'estrade (6) — 6 images

Où : écran du héros. Priorité ★★☆.

#### Habillage : boutons, cadres, plaques, barres — 3 images

Où : toute l'interface. Priorité ★★★.

Début du prompt, commun à la famille :

```
Hand-painted isometric game asset in exactly the art style of the first reference image (an Egyptian market stall): clean dark-brown ink outlines, flat painted textures with soft shading and subtle gradients, warm earthy palette with a few saturated accents, small lively details, 2:1 isometric projection viewed from the front at 30 degrees of elevation, light coming from the upper left. No text, no letters, no watermark, no ground tile under the subject. The image must have a fully transparent background (alpha channel): absolutely nothing painted around the subject, no backdrop, no vignette, no glow, no halo, no gradient. Only the subject and its small cast shadow on the right are opaque.
Repaint the subject shown in the second image (a plain 3D render used as a layout guide). Keep exactly the same camera angle, the same position in the frame, the same footprint, proportions and overall silhouette as the second image. Do not add anything outside its silhouette except small props at its base and a soft cast shadow to the right.
Composition: the element fills the whole square image edge to edge, front view, no perspective.
```
